### Inputs and work actually completed

- Created AGENTS.md, PRODUCT.md, DESIGN.md, ARCHITECTURE.md, ACCEPTANCE_TESTS.md, and BUILD_LOG.md through the patch tool. Existing blueprint/compliance/unrelated projects were preserved.

### Chosen small architecture and scope review

- One operator/testnet contract and one local runner; no multi-tenant service, database/indexer, hosted signer, swap/bridge, or paid AI dependency.
- Fund capsule plus separate bounded storage capacity; immutable owner policy/provider recipient/canonical quote; direct agent account/key and one-method gas-limited key.
- Pending approval does not hold funds/quotes. Exact approval re-checks and atomically reserves/consumes/schedules; no standalone generic pay API or hard-denial exception.
- Native callback success/failure resolves once; unknown/callback catastrophe stays pending/quarantined. Evidence-backed catastrophic recovery is deliberately not invented.
- Safe owner close returns unused purchase funds, separately from settled spending, after no pending operations; storage/history funding remains disclosed.
- Fixed 20-source JSON/syntax validator with explicit owner attestation; no external-truth/independent oracle/delivery/refund guarantee.
- Receipts keep transaction, Gate decision, settlement, and validation separate. Public app carries no shared agent key; actual lab/workflow executions use approved local commands.
- Cost per successful run includes settled costs of completed failed/error runs in the same cohort; no-success/unavailable is explicit.
- Roster quality/storytelling adapted to RunLatch; signature dither hero plus transition/data/quiet interface moments; static SVG/CSS first; no blanket Motion/Recharts/TanStack installs.
- Optional compiler, suggestions, reputation, notifications, recurring collection, commercial adapters, cross-chain/private inference, and complex visuals removed from mandatory scope and represented only as conditional cases.

No NASM/system installer, SDK downgrade, assembly disabling, or machine-security change; no effect on the WASM backend. Initial29 SDK/skeleton tests passed.

M2 **60 LOCAL tests passed**, formatting and strict Clippy passed. Corrected Clippy's constant-chunk suggestion with supported as_chunks; no lint suppression.

### M3 funds implementation and observed tests

- Added actual submit_purchase: zero deposit, exact runtime account/public key, canonical stored quote/policy checks. Authorized bounded denials persist; replay returns a rejected attempt referencing the unchanged original without duplicate payment.
- Added exact owner approve/reject/cancel; approvals re-check current deadline/hash/policy/quote/provider/capsule/budget. Pending approval holds no funds or quote.
- Authorized transfers reserve/consume before scheduling. Private callbacks read exactly one bounded native-transfer result. Success moves R to spent; confirmed failure releases R while keeping the quote consumed; unknown/missing/oversized results remain pending/quarantined. No public outcome setter or unsafe retry.
- Owner close/refund derives unused funding from stored allocation and pays only the stored owner, after no pending purchase/refund. Successful return adjusts net allocation/returned, not provider spend. Failed returns stay non-authorizing and retry only as a fresh internal attempt; stale/unknown callbacks cannot resend or free money.
- Current SDK-host-mock suite **82 passed,0 failed**, including the26 pure Gate cases (not82+26). Tests inspect constructed canonical transfer/callback actions, cumulative reservations, replay/approval/boundaries, private callback access, normal/failed/unknown results, revocation interleaving, and owner-refund invariants. These are LOCAL SDK mocks, not actual receipt settlement on a node.
- Current `cargo fmt ... -- --check`, `cargo test --locked --manifest-path contracts/runlatch/Cargo.toml`, and `cargo clippy --locked --all-targets ... -- -D warnings` pass with the documented process-local Windows fallback. Corrected a manual saturating-balance lint; ledger accounting remains checked integer arithmetic.
- Current official WASM build passes with compiler/protocol checks and wasm-opt: **267039 bytes**, SHA-256 **C85A4E785E6DC52EFCD1C09F8C79F7BF0FC785E4D3CEDA2E0C0494945AD296AB**. Optional ABI generation skipped for development only. No deployment.

### Genuine remaining test-environment blocker

Native SDK tests now work, but the official near-sandbox node documents Linux x86/macOS ARM64 execution. This machine has no WSL/Docker/approved Linux test host. SDK mocks cannot verify actual asynchronous receipt failure/refund finality, callback gas exhaustion, or worst-case runtime storage/gas. Current callback25T gas and conservative pre-funded storage ceilings are preliminary, not measured node guarantees. Full M3 is not accepted until those checks run; do not simulate a chain and call it testnet.

Best supported solution: local WSL2 Ubuntu for isolated Sandbox tests, keeping source/test identities local. Enabling OS features/admin setup and any reboot must be explicitly agreed; no silent Windows feature/security changes. An existing approved Linux host is an alternative. No private source upload/public repo/CI or public deployment is implied.

Next: resolve supported Linux Sandbox environment, validate actual receipt/gas/failure cases, then continue remaining workflow/validator/receipt/UI/dithercn work. Ordinary implementation remains autonomous; only genuine environment/external-action gates require the human.

## 84. Final-wait send path and the limit it exposed - 11 November

- Replaced the opaque `broadcast_tx_async` acknowledgement in the restricted purchase path with exactly one `send_tx` call using `wait_until: FINAL`, same signed bytes, no retry, no fallback and no second endpoint. The node now returns a real validation error or a FINAL call outcome instead of a bare hash acknowledgement. Files: `scripts/near/local-broadcast-exercise.ts` (`SendMode = "ack" | "final"`, separate 16 KiB/15 s ack and 256 KiB/60 s final bounds), `scripts/near/restricted-testnet-runner.ts` (final mode, 45 s default / 60 s ceiling), `scripts/near/single-attempt.ts` (LOCAL stays 15 s; only the admitted testnet path may wait longer).
- RPC text handling tightened at the same time: only allowlisted protocol identifiers (`RPC_NAMES`, `ERROR_TYPES`) and structural failure paths (up to four object keys) are retained; free-form `error_message`, logs, values and the nested free-form panic text are dropped. A `PRIVATE_CANARY` test proves no untrusted text reaches any result.
- `ACKNOWLEDGED_UNVERIFIED` is no longer accepted for a final-wait send; accepted broadcast statuses are `FINAL_OUTCOME_OBSERVED`, `SEND_ERROR_OBSERVED` (documented as node refusal, not proof of global non-execution) and the retained legacy `ACKNOWLEDGED_UNVERIFIED`. `payment_evidence` stays `NOT_OBSERVED` in every case: a FINAL function-call outcome is not settlement, because the scheduled native transfer and private callback are separate receipts.
- Tests: new case "final-wait send exposes validation errors and outcomes once, never as settlement" (validation error / SuccessValue / Failure, one call each, no canary leak, no settlement claim); transport fixtures updated to the FINAL result shape. `test:near` **306/306 pass**, `typecheck` and `lint` exit 0.
- Fixed a non-hermetic unit test: `readDemoDbIntent` falls back to the real `.local/testnet-mvp/single-attempt/working-mvp/` journal, which now exists because of the real attempt, so "readback-only never yields an intent" failed for environmental reasons. `readDemoDbIntent(directory, journalRoot?)` now accepts an explicit root and the test passes an empty directory. No production default changed.
- **Important limit found while preparing a second attempt (no signing, no send):** a repeat signed `submit_purchase` with the same restricted key cannot be produced by the existing safety layer, and must not be forced. `coordinator.lastIdentity()` returns the retained signed identity; the runner then requires `readSignedAgentNonce`, which calls `reconcileAgentNonce` and `assertTerminalPurchaseEvidence`, i.e. a FINAL on-chain prior transaction plus same-block key nonce. With the first transaction unrecorded, that fails closed at `freshReview` before any claim, signature or transport call. The sequential key chain also refuses slot 1 without that issued reconciliation and requires a different capsule+request, and the one-shot anchor blocks a second bootstrap for the same key. A new namespace, edited marker, reset, expiry release or recovery/owner key would each be a bypass, so none was added.
- Read-only confirmation just now: `node scripts/setup/human-db-purchase.ts --check` returned the same retained intent `3N98awqyrxUbGqA7zGVsZtpT28pmdJf5j2cdXR8c3GkP`, `submission UNAVAILABLE`, `payment_evidence SETTLEMENT_UNVERIFIED`, `limitation TESTNET_RPC_HTTP_ERROR`, no decision/receipt/transfer/callback/final record, no key load or send. Combined with the earlier FastNEAR, official NEAR RPC and NEAR Blocks v3 `UNKNOWN_TRANSACTION` reads, a definitive answer has still never been observed.
- Consequence for the approved "Option A" (same request ID, same quote, same nonce): it is not executable without violating the no-resend invariant just described, and expiry alone is not a release. The honest remaining choices are (C) demo the verified setup plus the explicitly unresolved purchase and the LOCAL Sandbox agency workflow, or (A') a genuinely new purchase authority - new quote/capsule/request plus a second restricted key or agent account - which needs new human signatures and new scope code. Nothing was signed, sent, deleted or released for this finding.
